- **rebase**: Rebases a feature branch onto its latest base, resolving conflicts while preserving the intent of local changes, optionally force-pushing and watching CI afterward. TRIGGER when: the user wants to rebase a feature branch, update a branch against its base, or replay local work on top of the latest upstream. DO NOT TRIGGER when: merging a release branch into dev → merging-branches; only watching CI on an already-open PR → monitoring-pull-requests.
